== firmware DEV190806042
make: ingresso nella directory «/opt/devel/mpy-lvgl-stm32/mpy-lvgl-boards/.work/micropython/ports/stm32»
Use make V=1 or set BUILD_VERBOSE in your environment to increase build verbosity.
Including User C Module from /opt/devel/mpy-lvgl-stm32/mpy-lvgl-boards/.work/lvgl-micropython
lvgl-micropython: no jpegio in this build -- lv.image will not draw a JPEG.
jpegio owns the TJpgDec decoder; PNG and LVGL BIN still decode.
Name jpegio as well for JPEG (build_mp.py --modules jpegio,lvgl-micropython).
Including User C Module from /opt/devel/mpy-lvgl-stm32/mpy-lvgl-boards/modules/st_ltdc

Use make V=1 or set BUILD_VERBOSE in your environment to increase build verbosity.
Including User C Module from /opt/devel/mpy-lvgl-stm32/mpy-lvgl-boards/.work/lvgl-micropython
lvgl-micropython: no jpegio in this build -- lv.image will not draw a JPEG.
jpegio owns the TJpgDec decoder; PNG and LVGL BIN still decode.
Name jpegio as well for JPEG (build_mp.py --modules jpegio,lvgl-micropython).

Use make V=1 or set BUILD_VERBOSE in your environment to increase build verbosity.
Including User C Module from /opt/devel/mpy-lvgl-stm32/mpy-lvgl-boards/.work/lvgl-micropython
lvgl-micropython: no jpegio in this build -- lv.image will not draw a JPEG.
jpegio owns the TJpgDec decoder; PNG and LVGL BIN still decode.
Name jpegio as well for JPEG (build_mp.py --modules jpegio,lvgl-micropython).
